### ⚠️ Identifier collision — `C-nn` means different things in the two streams

**Both streams use `C-nn` identifiers, for unrelated things, and this file now contains both.** Grepping an ID here returns the wrong artifact.

| ID | In the programme / `PBDIGIT-48` content below | In IERVP |
|---|---|---|
| **`C-13`** | a CCP-1 change item, and separately the `identifier-check.php` advisory gate (`:584`) | **a parked method candidate** — *"coverage is descriptive, completeness is normative"* |

**IERVP's `C-n` candidates live in `docs/publicdigit/backlog/README.md`, not here.** Same class as the `R-89`/`R-90` collision that backlog already records as precedent. **No renumbering is proposed** — renaming another stream's identifiers, or my own after they are cited in commits, would break traceability. **The collision is recorded so it is not mistaken for agreement.**

### 🔀 One known cross-stream discrepancy — recorded, NOT reconciled

**The rule *"coverage is descriptive, completeness is normative"* is `BOUND` in the `PBDIGIT-48` execution plan (commit `5e9dc5f5`) and `PARKED` as candidate `C-13` in the IERVP backlog.** **One rule, two governance states, one repository.**

**This may be entirely legitimate** — two streams governed by their own adopted rules. **It is recorded rather than resolved**, so a reader finding both states does not assume one is an error, and so neither stream silently adopts the other's position. **Reconciling it is a Product Owner act.** Full note: `docs/publicdigit/backlog/README.md`.

GOVERNANCE CORRECTION recorded: the first issue said 'G-1 RESOLVED' while also recording two pending ratifications -- those cannot both be true. Sequence is analysis -> recommended realization -> ratification -> RESOLUTION, never the reverse; an analysis cannot ratify itself (same class as 'readiness is evidence, acceptance is authority', which I applied to WP-6 in the same session and then failed to apply to my own recommendation).** **FOUR-LEVEL MODEL ADOPTED (reusable): Business Policy (Q-2/ARB) -> Architectural Invariant (ARB) -> Mechanism (engineering, the ONLY substitutable level) -> Implementation. It turns 'is this substitutable?' into a LOOKUP; all of G-1 was a level-3 substitution, which is why it dissolved without touching the model -- had it been level 2, the honest answer would have been to return to the ARB.** G-1 reclassified from 'engineering placement' to an **ARCHITECTURE-ENFORCEMENT ALIGNMENT GAP** (architecture correct, enforcement correct, the MAPPING incomplete) -- **the reclassification changed the answer, not just the label**: 'placement' invites 'put the file where Deptrac doesn't look' (option a: unguarded, and it would LOOK like compliance). **THE UNLOCK: mechanism vs invariant.** 'MAD has exactly one home' is the INVARIANT (AP-2, preserved absolutely); *'consume Adjudication's existing port'* was a **plan-level MECHANISM, never an architectural decision**. **RESOLUTION (option d): Election declares its OWN consumer-side port** -- `EvidencePreservationDurations` in Election/Application/Port/ + `ConfiguredEvidencePreservationDurations` in Election/Infrastructure/Config/, **mirroring ConfiguredAdjudicationDurations exactly** (injected Config, precedence, fail-closed), reading **the one canonical MAD key**. No cross-context import anywhere. **MAD is not Adjudication's data -- it is Q-2's POLICY housed in config/adjudication.php; both contexts are downstream of governance, not of each other.** Options (b) genuine TP-1 violation and (c) relax Deptrac both rejected -- **(c) not needed, so not proposed.** **🔴 R-D1 honest cost:** precedence logic would exist twice and could DRIFT -> recommended gate asserting both adapters resolve the same MAD for the same (electionType, organisationId), **protecting AP-2's INTENT not its letter**; extracting to Shared on first repetition DECLINED. **PLACEMENT PRINCIPLE: coverage follows MEANING, not the reverse** -- VO/port/service/adapter are gated because they carry policy; the folder parser, traversal, deletion and CLI stay ungated **correctly, because they carry none**. **RESPONSIBILITY INVENTORY (8 rows) replaces the rule inventory** and revealed that **R2 (policy ownership) and R7 (infrastructure) are the SAME defect from both ends -- policy ownership is violated the moment infrastructure decides a value, which is exactly AP-1; so C-1 is THE gate, not one of three.** R4/R5 likewise one drift, two symptoms. **Coverage 4/11 -> 5/11 (C-6 becomes actively enforced); alignment makes existing gates APPLY, it does not add gates.** **⚖️ TWO ARB RATIFICATIONS PENDING, neither reassigning a responsibility: A-1 mechanism substitution (confirm the INVARIANT, not the sentence, was binding) · A-2 boundary sharpening (Election ANSWERS, Audit/Retention ACTS). If A-1 declined, fallback is option (c) -- relaxing a correct gate, which must be a deliberate recorded act.** **RED blocked by THREE items: (1) A-1/A-2 ratification -- the act that RESOLVES G-1 · (2) WP-6 acceptance (programme) · (3) C-1 automation (deliverable inside 7A).** **📄 THE LAYER VERIFICATION RULE IS PROMOTED OUT OF WP-7 into a platform methodology module: engineering/knowledge/methodology/Layer_Verification_Rule.md** (beside DDD_Tactical_Governance_Principles.md -- that module governs WHAT TO BUILD, this one governs WHO MAY CHANGE IT AND AT WHICH LEVEL). Includes **the DUAL** (*if changing this layer forces a change above it, you have found an ARCHITECTURAL dependency, not an implementation one* -- so **a failing rule ESCALATES, it does not block**) and **the PROPOSAL CHECKLIST** (which layer changes / which higher layer would also change / escalate before implementation). **STATUS: PROPOSED, NOT ADOPTED -- non-binding until an explicit Decision Authority ruling (R-34); the artifact requesting indexing does not index itself. ## PKS governance framework — STABILIZED (2026-07-30); primary effort returns to architectural work
- **Declared stabilized after a governance coherence pass found NO conceptual conflicts** (one editorial gap only — the Discipline lacks the explicit `Lifecycle status` field its siblings carry; recorded, deliberately unfixed, because *a stabilization pass that starts editing is no longer a verification*).
- **Governance-state vocabulary CANONICAL — Discipline Amendment 2, two ORTHOGONAL axes:** **Axis A control admission** (Candidate → Provisional → Governed; exits **Declined** = never admitted, **Withdrawn** = admitted then removed by contrary evidence) · **Axis B artifact lifecycle** (Authored → Issued → Proposed → Adopted → Binding) · cross-cutting **Deferred / Superseded**. **A Provisional control may live in a Proposed document — the AFR does.** **The verb varies lawfully by subject: methodology *certified* · rule *adopted* · review contract *recognized* — one state, three verbs, not three states.**
- **CONTRACT ADMISSION RULE (Discipline Amendment 3, GOVERNED at n=4): *artifact kinds may define review EMPHASES; review CONTRACTS require evidence of a capability existing contracts cannot provide.*** Four concerns separated: artifact kind ≠ contract ≠ emphasis ≠ capability test. **Contracts: KCR · AFR only.** Emphasis construct **PROVISIONAL at n=1** (publication → obj 7 · 1 · 11); **promotion needs CROSS-CONTEXT evidence — a different artifact category or review situation — since two publication emphases would be repetition within one lineage (R-M6-6/T-2).** ***Usefulness is evidence for an emphasis; unique capability is evidence for a contract.*** ***A distinct lens is not a distinct capability. A named emphasis costs nothing; a named contract costs a governance construct.***
- **Standing consequence: governance evolves only when operational evidence demands it.** Four framework layers frozen; admission ladder governs additions; **most future filter tests should return "no addition needed."** **No further governance commission is recommended — the next substantive act is architectural (AFV-F4).**

- **🟩 PHASE II.C C4 ARCHITECTURE VIEWS EXECUTED (C4-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_IIC_C4_Architecture_Views.md`.** Treated as a **knowledge-preserving transformation**, not diagramming: the views *communicate* architecture and do not create it; **they are representations — not evidence, not decisions, not design.** Governing principle enforced throughout: **visual precision ≤ architectural precision; silence over invention.** **Three views rendered: L1 System Context · L2 Container View · a Responsibility Allocation View SUBSTITUTING for L3** (Exception Protocol invoked — AD-1 allocates responsibilities but derives no internal components, so nested boxes would assert structure that does not exist); **L4 not derivable, not supplied.** **THREE REQUESTED ITEMS FOUND NON-DERIVABLE AND RECORDED RATHER THAN SUPPLIED: (1) ACTORS — AD-1 derives none, and more strongly the governed model records ABSENT ROLES (two ownership vacuums; no owner for the capability AC-1 encloses, OQ-PKS-3). Drawing actors would invent roles the corpus records as missing — the most consequential available act of invention, and the one most likely to pass unnoticed in a diagram. (2) MECHANISM on any arrow** — all eight arrows carry **direction and content only**; nothing about synchronicity, messaging, events, request/response, orchestration, choreography, execution order, transport, or delivery (AD-1 §9.2). **(3) A component decomposition.** **7 diagram principles (DP-1..DP-7)**, incl. **DP-6: containers carry NO runtime/deployment semantics** (the C4 container notion's runtime connotation is explicitly disclaimed — AD-1 is deployment-neutral) and **DP-7: the views are subordinate to what they render** — *"nothing may cite a view as authority"* applies to these diagrams; on conflict **AD-1 governs and the diagram is corrected**. Visual enforcement of the architecture's sharp constraints: **AC-2 rendered as a terminal sink (no arrow leaves it, DR-1)** · the issuance-trigger arrow **originates in an undefined region** (AC-1 cannot self-issue) · AC-1's outbound surface narrower than its internal vocabulary · A8 one-way inward only (DR-5) · AC-2↔XD-1 shown as an explicit non-relationship, not an arrow. **9 intentional omissions recorded (KO-1..KO-9), every one tracing to something the GOVERNED MODEL leaves open and none to a diagramming limitation** — the exact failure mode the commission existed to prevent (completing a design by drawing it). **All five fidelity gates verified** (Architectural · Semantic · Governance · Knowledge · Boundary). **Coherence check passed and recorded: the Phase II.C output is itself an instance of CBC-2's membership rule** — a derived artifact carrying no independent authority. **Promotion recommendation: DP-1/DP-2/DP-4 are candidates for promotion BEYOND this program** as general knowledge-engineering constraints on visual representation (demonstrated, not asserted) — but **not** as methodology changes; the PMR → MCA → CDR path governs any elevation. **KO-1 flagged as the most consequential omission:** an architecture that cannot show who operates it is a finding about the domain's ownership vacuums, not a diagramming deficiency.

Root cause recorded: **ordinary prose-smoothing, a stylistic impulse with a semantic cost — in a frozen-terminology regime the governed term must survive editing for readability.** **VR-1/VR-3/VR-4 (Minor):** three labels sourced directly from M6/M8 rather than through AD-1 (exceeding the declared input set; content governed and accurate, so the defect is *routing*) · the AR negation list's provenance (the commission text) unrecorded · DR-2's non-rendering not listed among intentional omissions. **Shared root cause found: C4-1 recorded provenance for its ELEMENTS but not consistently for its LABELS, ANNOTATIONS, and OMISSIONS — the gap DP-5-as-originally-written permitted, which the PA's strengthening independently closes (refinement and findings identified the same weakness).** **Verified clean:** no orphan object · no invented boundary or dependency · no mechanism on any of the eight arrows · **DR-1 visually enforced (no arrow leaves AC-2)** · DR-3..DR-8 reflected · undefined/candidate/absent/contested all preserved (and rendered *more* visibly than convention would, as explicit negative markers — since silence about an absence is indistinguishable from oversight). **Notable clarification recorded:** the A1/A2 pair between AR-1 and AC-1 **looks like a cycle and is not one** (distinct dependencies on distinct content; - **🟦 KNOWLEDGE BASELINE INTEGRITY REVIEW EXECUTED (KBI-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Knowledge_Baseline_Integrity_Review.md`.** PA ruling adopted: **promotion should not follow C4-2 directly** — Phase II is now large enough that *consistency between artifacts* matters more than reviewing them individually; the question becomes *do these artifacts collectively describe one coherent body of knowledge?* **VERDICT: the baseline is COHERENT — one strategic model, one architecture, one representation, with terminology, epistemic classes, governance boundaries, and traceability intact across all six artifacts plus the binding governance record. 5 findings: 2 Moderate · 3 Minor · 0 Major. Two are visible ONLY at baseline level.** **KBI-F1 (Moderate — the principal finding): verification coverage is ASYMMETRIC across the transformation chain.** Discovery→Model has dedicated audits (Critical Review · Evidence Restatement · Validation Review); Architecture→Representation has one (C4-2, element by element); **the Model→Architecture link has NONE — AD-1's derivation fidelity rests on self-verification, the instrument RET-1 records as 0-for-2 on defects later stages found, and it is the link where the MOST interpretation occurs.** Recommendation (Authority's choice): commission an Architecture Fidelity Verification, **or** disclose the asymmetry in promotion materials — *acceptable if disclosed, misleading if not*; invisible to a reader of either artifact alone. **KBI-F2 (Minor): AD-1 minted five identifier namespaces without declaration** (AC- · AR- · SB-/IB- · AP- · DR-) while the PMR register — created later — *did* declare its own; and **"AD-" already denotes architecture-debt items (AD-M1/AD-M2) in this project's working state.** No literal collision (AD-1 ≠ AD-M1) but precisely M4's risk class (*collisions occur exactly where register discipline lapses*). **KBI-F3 (Minor):** AD-1 declares RET-1 an input with no traced contribution. **KBI-F4 (Minor, carried): M7's three DAR-1 editorial applications remain PENDING** — M7's text still names two withdrawn patterns (the decision governs on conflict, and M8/AD-1/C4-1 all follow the decision, but M7 alone would mislead). Plus VR-1..VR-4 carried, still open. **VERIFIED CLEAN:** one architecture across three artifacts, no contradiction · **all epistemic classes hold — incl. the subtle case examined rather than assumed: C4-1's DP-1..DP-7 are derived restatements binding only their own commission, not governance acts** · no frozen decision touched · **no hidden governance decision** (checked specifically: AD-1's DRs are *architecture* rules, not program governance) · **configuration control tested FIVE times and held five times** (PMR-1 · PMR-2 · the §4.4 observation · DAR-1's ground 2 · RR-1..RR-9 — two originating with the Authority itself) · no intentionally-unresolved item resolved downstream. **One chain spot-checked end-to-end through six artifacts with no drift** ("criterion — used here, owned elsewhere"). **PROMOTION READINESS (baseline level): NOT YET READY — three artifacts need editorial restoration first (M7 · AD-1 · C4-1); five are Promotion Ready; NONE is "Governance Review Required" or "Not Ready".** **Finding pattern recorded: every finding is in a *recording* or *coverage* layer — none in the substance; no artifact says a wrong thing about the domain** (mirroring RET-1's central property). **Candidate methodology instrument recorded + routed, not added:** *every transformation link should carry a named verification, and the absence of one should be visible* — KBI-F1 was invisible to five artifact-level reviews and appeared immediately when coverage was mapped per *link*. **Closing statement recorded: KBI-1 attests INTERNAL coherence only — a perfectly coherent baseline built by a single lineage is exactly as strong as its evidence and no stronger.**
- **🟥 ARCHITECTURE FIDELITY VERIFICATION EXECUTED (AFV-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_IIB_Architecture_Fidelity_Verification.md`.** Commissioned to close KBI-F1's assurance gap; **conformed mid-execution to the PA's refined commission**, which relocates the object of verification: **the target is the TRANSFORMATION, not the destination artifact** (*is AD-1 a **lawful transformation** of the Strategic Model?* not *is AD-1 correct?*). Inputs restricted to **M0–M8 · DAR-1 · RET-1 · AD-1**; **C4-1, C4-2, KBI-1 deliberately excluded as downstream** (downstream artifacts cannot verify upstream transformations). **RUNNING DISCIPLINE recorded: architecture *correctness* (internally consistent?) is OUT OF SCOPE; only *fidelity* (faithfully preserves the model?) was asked — and AD-1 is internally consistent at all four points where fidelity fails, so a correctness audit would have passed every one of them.** **VERDICT: the transformation is LAWFUL IN STRUCTURE and DEFECTIVE IN FOUR STEPS.** **§10A Transformation Integrity (the central objective) tested three questions separately:** **(1) refinement not redesign — PASSES DECISIVELY** via a cardinality test no artifact-level review performs: the mapping from disposed strategic elements to architectural elements is **BIJECTIVE** (CBC-1→AC-1 · CBC-2→AC-2 · CBC-4→XD-1 · CBC-3→AR-1 · core→AR-2), with the three contested concepts and the preserved partition carried in exactly the state the model left them — nothing added, dropped, split, or merged; **(2) derivation not interpretation — MIXED**, derivational in structure but interpretive at four points; **(3) transformation not extension — ONE BREACH, one borderline.** **FINDINGS (classes per the refined list): AFV-F1 Transformation** (reclassified from Semantic) — *"AC-1 cannot self-issue"* converts a **role** separation (author vs gate-or-review) into a **context-boundary** claim; **M6 §3.1's (c) columns flag exactly this alternative for L1-4 AND L1-13**; also internally inconsistent with AP-1's own first clause (a Verdict is knowledge, so issuing is not an authority act) · **AFV-F2 Semantic** — DR-1 widens *"as authority"* to *"as authority **or as evidence"*** without deriving it (a sound L4-8 derivation was available and unused) · **AFV-F3 Semantic** — AP-3 makes forward-only revision a **binding principle** although M6 §3.1 records its status as *"a constitutional property **or an unexamined habit**"*; AD-1's own AP-4 demonstrates the correct qualification pattern · **AFV-F4 GOVERNANCE (High)** — **AR-1 is placed wholly inside the PKS boundary unqualified, which presupposes OQ-PKS-7's one-corpus reading**; M6 §9 records that under the alternative *"part of the demoted seam's content is outside PKS"*, and OQ-PKS-7 appears nowhere in AD-1's inputs, principles, or six open questions · **AFV-F5 Editorial** — "Normative region" drops "Governance" from the governed name. **Zero Traceability findings, zero Coverage findings, zero orphan architecture.** **METHOD FINDING (the most useful result): three of five findings came from the strategic model's own Phase-B½ (b)/(c) characterization columns — AD-1 derived from the (a) columns without consulting what the model said it did NOT say and which alternatives were live. Every over-strengthening was pre-flagged by the model itself, one work package earlier, in columns built precisely to prevent this.** **Why no earlier gate caught it: AD-1's quality gates verified *traceability* (does every element trace?) and all did — but traceability asks *is there a source?* while fidelity asks *does the source bear this weight?*** **CONSEQUENCE FOR PROMOTION: AD-1 is reclassified GOVERNANCE REVIEW REQUIRED (superseding any earlier editorial-only classification) — AFV-F4's remedy is an Authority disposition, not a fix, though M6 §9 already supplies the exact qualifying wording.** All four other findings are bounded editorial. **The refined STOP honored: findings recorded only; the one methodology-adjacent observation is explicitly NOT proposed, NOT routed, and requests no register entry.**
- **🟧 AUTHORITY IMPACT ASSESSMENT EXECUTED (AIA-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Authority_Impact_Assessment.md`.** PA ruling adopted: **do NOT jump from AFV-1 into editing** — the governance path has forked into two classes (Authority disposition vs editorial restoration) that must not be mixed, because **editors could unintentionally resolve an Authority-owned decision** ("governance contamination"). Sequence made explicit: **Authority Impact Assessment → Authority Disposition → Editorial Restoration → Promotion Readiness Verification → Promotion** (Authority does not directly trigger editing; it first establishes what editing is permitted). Inputs limited to AFV-1 · AD-1 · M0–M8 · DAR-1; C4 corrections/promotion artifacts excluded (C4-1 examined only as an *object of impact*). **HEADLINE (AIA-F1, MAJOR — must be addressed BEFORE the disposition, not merely before editing): the three outcomes are ASYMMETRIC and the asymmetry runs OPPOSITE TO INTUITION. ACCEPT is the NARROW act — it qualifies the placement with wording M6 §9 already supplies, restores OQ-PKS-7 to open in the architecture, and DECIDES NOTHING about the open question. REJECT is the BROAD act — leaving the unqualified placement standing is architecturally equivalent to ADOPTING the one-corpus reading, i.e. a substantive resolution of an ARB-owned question by inaction, requiring its own record + impact statement + ARB notice. DEFER decides nothing and blocks baseline promotion indefinitely.** *If the Authority treats REJECT as the conservative default, it will resolve an open question by declining to act.* **AIA-F2 (Moderate): the disposition PROPAGATES beyond AD-1 — C4-1's Level 2 renders AR-1 inside the system boundary, so ACCEPT creates a FIFTH C4-1 correction not among its current four; C4-2's PASS remains correct because the defect reached C4-1 by INHERITANCE, not representation error.** **AIA-F3 (Minor, procedural): AFV-F5 edits AR-1's label — the very element under review — so it is SEQUENCE-DEFERRED into the post-disposition act (AR-1 edited exactly once).** **AIA-F4 (Minor, bounding + positive): the strategic layer never contained the presupposition** (M8 carries OQ-PKS-7 with its impact statement intact) — **so no outcome implies re-verification of M0–M8 and no strategic disposition is at risk.** **EDITORIAL AUTHORIZATION MATRIX (the commission's operational purpose): of eleven open corrections — 3 UNAFFECTED and may proceed independently (M7's DAR-1 folds) · 7 AUTHORIZED with low contamination risk (AFV-F1/F2/F3, KBI-F3, VR-1..VR-4; KBI-F2 authorized with one constraint — the namespace declaration must not characterize AR-1's placement) · 1 SEQUENCE-DEFERRED (AFV-F5) · 2 do not yet exist (created only by ACCEPT). NOTHING is permanently blocked; the contamination surface is ONE correction wide.** **CERTIFICATION STATE UNAFFECTED** — a fidelity defect in an artifact produced *under* the frozen method is not a defect *in* the method. **ADVISORY (advisory only): ACCEPT** — narrow, no new knowledge, every strategic boundary preserved, radius of two artifacts; if REJECT is preferred, the record must state the OQ-PKS-7 adoption explicitly; **and whatever the outcome, the disposition should state what it does NOT decide** (OQ-PKS-7 · CBC-3's status · the seam's promotion · the partition's re-entry), since over-reading the disposition is the likeliest downstream error. One methodology-adjacent observation recorded **without routing** (the SDM has no rule requiring architectural derivations to carry unresolved-OQ contingencies forward).
- **🟨 CONTROLLED CHANGE PLAN EXECUTED (CCP-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Controlled_Change_Plan.md`.** PA ruling adopted: **AIA-1 asked *what becomes authorized*; CCP-1 asks *what exactly must change*** — different questions, and without CCP editors would implicitly decide edit order, dependency order, stopping conditions, and propagation, **which are architectural decisions**. **§0 records a sequencing tension in the commission itself and resolves it: the commission's diagram places CCP-1 after the disposition, but the disposition is still pending and no disposition record exists — so the plan is written OUTCOME-CONDITIONALLY across ACCEPT / REJECT / DEFER, which is strictly stronger** (a plan written after ACCEPT would silently omit the REJECT branch's obligations). **No disposition assumed, implied, or recommended.** **PLAN: 16 change items · 3 artifacts changed (AD-1 · C4-1 · M7) + 1 Authority record under REJECT · 5 bounded work packages · 8 objective exit criteria.** **Outcome dependency: ACCEPT → all 16 · REJECT → 14 + C-17 (the Authority record adopting the one-corpus reading, per AIA-F1) with C-01/C-14 void · DEFER → AD-1 partial only, C-01/C-05/C-14 held, baseline not promotable.** **TWO DERIVED ORDERING RULES an editor should not have to invent: (1) AD-1 BEFORE C4-1, always — a representation may never be edited ahead of its source, or the drift C4-2 just removed is re-introduced and C-14 becomes unverifiable (the transformation direction dictates the edit direction, not preference); (2) AD-1 amended EXACTLY ONCE — Package B is unblocked and lawful to run early, but waiting costs nothing downstream since AD-1's promotion blocker is the *disposition*, not the corrections (trade-off stated, not hidden: if DEFER persists, release Package B on its own rather than hold it hostage).** **Package E (M7's three DAR-1 folds) MAY PROCEED IMMEDIATELY — wholly independent, parallel-safe with everything.** **§5.2 names SIX artifact classes explicitly NOT changed, each with its reason — the section that most protects the baseline: KBI-1 is SUPERSEDED not corrected (forward-only supersession, L1-7 — a prior review is never edited to agree with a later one; and KBI-1's caution was correct) · C4-2's PASS STANDS (C4-1 faithfully rendered AD-1 as written; the defect arrived by inheritance) · AFV-1/AIA-1 untouched (findings apply to the OBJECT, never to the record that raised them) · M0–M6/M8/DAR-1 untouched (AIA-F4: the strategic layer never held the presupposition) · MCA/CDR/Checkpoint/RET-1 untouched (certification unaffected) · PMR register untouched.** *Left unstated, an editor could reasonably "tidy" KBI-1's superseded classification or annotate C4-2's verdict — either would breach forward-only supersession or contradict a correct verification.* **7 CONFIGURATION-INTEGRITY RULES incl. CI-1 NO EDITOR-AUTHORED SUBSTANCE — and §7.1 verifies wording is PRE-SUPPLIED for all 16 items (M6 §9 · AFV-1 §§6.1/7.1/8.1/11 · the PMR register's precedent form · KBI-1 §5 · C4-2 §9 · DAR-1 §§5–7), which makes CI-1 enforceable rather than aspirational. ESCALATION RULE: if pre-supplied wording does not fit the target text, STOP AND ESCALATE — the mismatch is a finding, not an invitation to compose. That single rule keeps execution from becoming design.** **C-08 flagged as the ONLY item that ADDS content (an open question recording the issuance-placement uncertainty) — conservative direction, wording constrained.** **C-11 (VR-2's Verdict restoration) carries its own exit criterion X-5 — the one item whose omission would propagate a governed-term drift into promotion.** **X-6 is the criterion that would catch this plan's target failure mode: a representation asserting more than its source after both were edited.**
- **🟥 EXECUTION READINESS VERIFICATION EXECUTED (ERV-1, PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Execution_Readiness_Verification.md`.** PA ruling adopted: **the plan itself needs independent verification** — CCP-1 was *authored* but never *challenged*, mirroring AFV-1's pattern (verify the transformation before trusting the architecture ⇒ verify the plan before trusting the implementation). Inputs: CCP-1 · AIA-1 · AFV-1 · AD-1 · DAR-1. **Method framing that produced the findings: read every change item as an instruction to a STRANGER, not as a reminder to its author.** **VERDICT: CONDITIONAL GO — 2 Major · 3 Moderate · 1 Minor. Packages A–D MUST NOT EXECUTE; Package E (M7's three DAR-1 folds) is CLEARED to proceed.** **ERV-F1 (MAJOR): three change items require the editor to CHOOSE between substantive alternatives** — C-03 (*narrow DR-1 **or** add the L4-8 derivation*) · C-05 (*governed name **or** architectural label*) · C-10 (*reword three labels **or** widen the input declaration*). **Each "or" is an architectural choice, defeating CCP-1's own CI-1 (no editor-authored substance) and the two-teams-identical-outcome criterion. Why CCP-1's own §7.1 check missed it: §7.1 asks *does wording exist?* — true for all 16 — but not *is the wording unique?* PRE-SUPPLIED ≠ DETERMINED.** C-03 is the sharpest: one option removes a prohibition, the other retains it with justification. **ERV-F2 (MAJOR): AFV-1 §13(4) explicitly recommended a bounded re-read of its four finding sections against the amended AD-1 — CCP-1 contains no such step, and Package D's exit criteria don't cover it. It is also an ORDERING gap: without the re-read between Package A+B and Package C, a mis-applied fidelity correction is faithfully rendered into C4-1** (the same inheritance pattern AIA-1 found for AR-1, this time with no downstream verification left to catch it). **ERV-F3 (Moderate): C-10's recommended remedy alters a COMMISSION-DERIVED constraint** (C4-1's "no additional sources permitted") — **not an editorial act**; defect inherited from C4-2's recommendation, not introduced by CCP-1, but owner class must be exact. **ERV-F4 (Moderate): the plan is SILENT ON ROLLBACK, which the commission requires addressed — and under CI-6 forward-only, rollback is genuinely UNAVAILABLE.** **ERV-F5 (Moderate): under DEFER, Package C's precondition ("A+B complete") is unsatisfiable-as-written since A doesn't run.** **ERV-F6 (Minor): C-09/C-15's history-row CONTENT unspecified — and C4-1's existing row ("corrections recommended, not applied here") would be left misleading.** **THE COMPOUNDING FINDING (the report's most important): ERV-F4 makes ERV-F1 worse — a plan with editor choices is tolerable if wrong choices can be reverted; under forward-only they CANNOT. In a forward-only regime DETERMINISM IS THE ONLY ERROR-PREVENTION MECHANISM AVAILABLE, so executing with ERV-F1 open means accepting a coin-flip on three architectural statements with no way back.** **CONFIRMED SOUND: finding coverage complete (only the recommended step omitted) · both ordering rules correct and genuinely derived · the six not-to-change classes verified item by item · outcome-conditional branching across all three dispositions (a genuine strength — a post-ACCEPT plan would have omitted C-17) · full traceability · 12 of 16 items deterministic as written · X-6 the strongest exit criterion, though it cannot detect a defect present in BOTH AD-1 and C4-1.** **CONDITIONS FOR GO: resolve ERV-F1 (select one wording per item, or escalate the selection) · ERV-F2 (insert the re-read as Package C's precondition) · ERV-F3 (reassign C-10's remedy or owner class); ERV-F4/F5/F6 recommended in the same amendment.** **Residual risk recorded: ERV-1 is the last link and HAS NO VERIFIER OF ITS OWN; and the chain has grown long — no link has verified whether its length now costs more than it returns (recorded as an observation, NOT proposed for action).**
- **⏸️ PROGRAM PARKED ON A STRATEGIC-BOUNDARY DECISION (PA, 2026-07-28 — no artifact produced; the PA is holding the next commission pending this answer).** Two PA points accepted: **(1) governance recursion is a real risk and the process needs a STOPPING RULE** — the PA and ERV-1 §11(3) converged independently on this; **(2) ERV-1's output is an ENGINEERING WORK PACKAGE, not another review** — Conditional GO with bounded defects means *amend CCP-1*, which is what ERV-1 §12(3) itself recommends; **no further verification commission is warranted.** **THE OPEN QUESTION (the PA's, and it is a strategic boundary decision, not an editorial one): does the methodology END at the certified governance baseline, or does it explicitly GOVERN THE TRANSITION into software implementation?** — because the answer determines whether the next artifact belongs to the **knowledge-governance** bounded context or marks the **handover** into the **implementation** bounded context. **Assistant's recorded recommendation (advisory; the decision is the PA's): the binary may be false — the record already contains the pattern for a third answer, a governed HANDOVER (one bounded transition artifact stating what implementation inherits, what remains open, and what it may not assume) without governing implementation itself, which is exactly what Consolidation §4 and M8 §11 did at every prior stage boundary. Supporting evidence: an implementation methodology ALREADY EXISTS and is separately frozen in this repository (Implementation Process v1.0, the 15-step workflow, 14-box DoD, with the EPIC-001..004 / PB-003..007 track running under it), so the transition target is not a vacuum; and AD-1's Q-1 marks the natural seam — it requires domain evidence about how the practiced system operates, a question the knowledge methodology cannot answer from its own corpus.** **Nothing is executed, amended, or promoted while parked. Package E remains cleared to proceed independently of this decision.**
- **🟩 CONTROLLED EXECUTION EXECUTED TO THE GATE (PA commission, 2026-07-28) — `docs/implementation/PKS_Phase_II_Execution_Record.md`.** PA decided the parked strategic-boundary question in favour of Option B, **named (ARB, 2026-07-28) IMPLEMENTATION HANDOVER GOVERNANCE** — *not* "governs the transition into implementation", which reads as continuation. **The governed chain is three-layered and the middle layer is the point: Knowledge Methodology → GOVERNED HANDOVER (a transition contract, functioning as an anticorruption boundary) → Implementation Methodology.** **ARB verdict: APPROVE.** Decisive reasoning recorded (the ARB's, sharper than the assistant's own emphasis): **because Implementation Process v1.0 already exists and is separately frozen, Option B is NOT the knowledge methodology expanding into implementation — it is governing the CONTRACT BETWEEN TWO METHODOLOGIES.** The methodology does not own implementation. **BINDING SCOPE GUARD on the future handover artifact (ARB): it MAY contain — the certified baseline · assumptions · unresolved questions · implementation constraints · the inheritance contract · explicit non-assumptions. It MAY NOT contain — aggregates · repositories · services · APIs · infrastructure · technologies. The moment it prescribes implementation structure it has crossed into Tactical DDD.** — and commissioned controlled execution with an explicit **STOPPING RULE: this was the LAST verification-before-execution commission; no new verification commission, finding class, or governance rule may be created.** Honored in full. **✅ ACT 1 — CCP-1 AMENDMENT 1 APPLIED (`...Controlled_Change_Plan.md` §11), discharging ERV-1's three blocking conditions plus the three advisory ones: ERV-F1 → the three undetermined items SELECTED with recorded rationale** (C-03 → *add the L4-8 derivation*, on minimal-change grounds — the defect was an absent derivation, not a wrong rule · C-05 → *use the governed name "Normative Governance region"*, on terminology-freeze grounds · C-10 → *reword the three labels*, as required by ERV-F3) **· ERV-F2 → NEW STEP C-18**, a bounded fidelity confirmation re-reading AFV-1 §§5.1/6.1/7.1/8.1 against the amended AD-1, **inserted as a PRECONDITION of Package C** (closes the ordering gap; a non-confirmation is a STOP-and-escalate) **· ERV-F3 → C-10 reassigned to the editorial remedy; C4-1's input declaration is NOT to be widened by any package · ERV-F4 → ROLLBACK STATED UNAVAILABLE** (supersession only; an unsure editor must escalate *before* editing, since there is no afterwards — this makes the escalation rule the primary control, not a fallback) **· ERV-F5 → under DEFER, Package C does NOT run · ERV-F6 → history-row content SPECIFIED. All 17 items are now DETERMINISTIC; no item offers the editor a choice.** **✅ ACT 2 — PACKAGE E EXECUTED (C-16) on M7:** the three DAR-1 folds applied — **R-1's Customer/Supplier name WITHDRAWN** (dependency + pattern undetermined) · **R-3b's "Separate Ways in the authority direction" REPLACED by a constraint statement** (Separate Ways now survives only at R-5) · **R-4's cross-edge Customer/Supplier name WITHDRAWN** (cross-edge dependency, no coordination pattern) — plus §1's summary bullets, two context-map annotations, R-3's internal sentence, and a history row carrying the §11.6 content (**explicitly recording that the application POST-DATES the Validation Review and DAR-1**, so the amended version cannot be mistaken for the verified one). **Exit check + all seven CI rules verified; nothing else in M7 differs** (every dependency, direction, ownership statement, evidence citation, grade, U-1..U-4, the §6.1 evidence matrix, and §7.3's observation stand as issued). **M7's promotion blocker is CLEARED (KBI-F4 discharged).** **⛔ PACKAGES A+B, C, D NOT EXECUTED — the AFV-F4 disposition DOES NOT EXIST.** Not a refusal and not a new finding: the precondition is recorded in the commission's own tables (*"AFV-F4 ⏳ pending"*; *"Package A+B: After AFV-F4 disposition"*) and in CCP-1 §10. **Two concrete harms avoided by stopping: (i) the single-amendment rule would break — C-01/C-05 exist only once the outcome is known, so AD-1 would be amended twice; (ii) governance contamination — working inside AD-1's AR-1 entry while its placement is under Authority review is precisely the scenario AIA-1 exists to prevent.** Per the plan's escalation rule, **execution stopped rather than proceeding on interpretation.** *Not claimed: that Package B is unlawful — AIA-1 authorized its six items, and CCP-1's release valve applies only if DEFER persists, which has not been ruled.*

ARB named its own decisive point: **"Implementation Process v1.0 already exists"** — which converts Option B from *expanding* the knowledge methodology into *governing the contract between two methodologies*. **ARB recommendation + caution ROUTED, NOT ADOPTED** (both are methodology changes; configuration control admits change only via execution evidence → MCA-class assessment → CDR-class decision): **PMR-3 — distinguish Category A permanent governance capabilities (Discovery · Architecture · Verification · Authority · Handover; AFV reads as core) from Category B situational execution controls (CCP · ERV)**, so future projects don't inherit every historical safeguard regardless of risk. **Evidence deliberately ASYMMETRIC and recorded as such: strong that these controls added value HERE (ERV-1 found 2 Major defects in CCP-1; AIA-1 found REJECT was the larger act; AFV-1 found 4 fidelity defects) — but n=0 on whether they add value in SIMPLER contexts.** **Two questions raised for assessment, not resolved: (1) WHO decides a change is "high-risk" enough to activate a Category-B control? It is the most implementation-relevant open item in the record, because a team MUST choose a coupling mechanism on day one; without it they convert an undetermined strategic question into a permanent technical decision, which is exactly what Section 1 defines the contract to prevent. IBC-1 fails its stated purpose at the one point where failure is certain rather than possible. DR-8, the most implementation-actionable) · OQ-PKS-1 missing · DD-1/DD-2 reasons factually wrong · "Certified" used for boundaries that are ACCEPTED (the VR-2 defect class under frozen terminology). **A constraint whose subject is unstated cannot be conformed to or audited — which makes §11's unenforceability a symptom, not a separate defect.** **STRONGEST ELEMENTS, recorded: §12's "does NOT inherit" list (authority · promotion · question-resolution · seam-promotion · decision rights) is what makes the semantic firewall real rather than aspirational · §7's FA-3 shows genuine understanding of AFV-F4 by forbidding the very assumption AD-1 makes unqualified · IC-2's explicit naming of OQ-PKS-7 is the best-designed constraint in the document · and the scope guard is DEFENDED not flagged: IC-1/2/3 name APIs/schemas/repos only to FORBID misuses, and naming a mechanism to forbid it is not prescribing it.** **Sharp observation recorded: §11 assigns conformance criteria to a capability the record says operates nowhere (T-17) and is unowned (OQ-PKS-3) — IBC-1 instantiates T-17 in its own enforcement clause.** **12 recommendations, all resolvable from material the record already holds; nothing requires redesign. - **⬛ ARB REVIEW DISCIPLINE ISSUED BY THE AUTHORITY (2026-07-28) — 7 rules, IN FORCE for every review of a Strategic DDD or governance artifact; recorded durably in `.claude/MEMORY.md`.** Distinguish constraint/property/mechanism · recommend constraints freely · **never prescribe implementation mechanisms** unless the certified architecture mandates them · express implementation expectations as **conformance requirements ("shall demonstrate")**, not realization techniques ("use ArchUnit/an ACL/separate schemas") · **classify every identifier mapping as authoritative / traceable / inferred / proposed** · preserve source-of-truth governance · scope-check each recommendation against Strategic-DDD/implementation-governance before issuing. **Scope note recorded: these are standing instructions on REVIEWER CONDUCT, in force immediately; binding them on future review COMMISSIONS (i.e. making them SDM/EOP requirements) is a methodology change and follows the PMR → MCA → CDR path — the same distinction applied to PMR-4.** **SELF-AUDIT APPLIED RETROACTIVELY to the IBC-1 review (§6 addendum) — and Rule 5 found a real defect in my own work: IBC-M11 ("five of eight dependency rules absent") CITED GOVERNED IDENTIFIERS but asserted a requirement that is PROPOSED, not governed — nothing in the record says a boundary contract must transmit all eight DRs. RECLASSIFIED Major → Recommendation. Derived, and this is why Rule 5 earns its place: CITING A GOVERNED IDENTIFIER DOES NOT MAKE THE REQUIREMENT GOVERNED — the same distinction ERV-1 drew between *pre-supplied* and *determined*, and AFV-1 between *traceability* and *fidelity*: a real source, correctly cited, supporting a claim it does not bear.** Mapping classes now supplied for all 21 findings (C1/C3/M3/M7/M8/M10 **authoritative** · C2 and most Majors **traceable** — C2's basis is entailment from IBC-1's own Section 1, the strongest non-authoritative basis available · M1 **mixed**, Major only on its DAR-1 limb · M11 **proposed**). **Revised count: 3 Critical · 10 Major · 5 Minor · 1 Recommendation · 2 Suggestions; verdict REVISE unchanged.** Rules 1/3/7 verified compliant (no tool, framework, technology, or realization technique named anywhere; Rule 1 was applied *against* an easy finding by defending IC-1/2/3 rather than flagging them); **Rule 4 produced new Recommendation 13** — restate Section 11's criteria in *"shall demonstrate"* form so each names a dischargeable obligation rather than a state observed by an unnamed observer; **Rule 6 was already satisfied** before issuance (H-1 held against the frozen source; PMR-5 registered).
- **⬛ ARB REVIEW DISCIPLINE EXTENDED — RULES 8–13 (Chief Architect, 2026-07-28; recorded in MEMORY, in force immediately, same scope note).** **(8)** every finding declares its **authority basis** (baseline / accepted-architecture / methodology violation · derived constraint · reviewer recommendation) — **constitutional weight derives from this, not from severity**; **(9)** every output declares its **constitutional category** (architecture defect · governance defect · methodology observation · improvement recommendation) — **severity and category are ORTHOGONAL, never merged into one severity list**; **(10)** proposed wording must be located as authoritative-source / assembly / explanatory prose — **only prose may be rewritten directly**; **(11)** ask *"what constitutional act would resolve this?"* — **if unclear, it is a QUESTION, not a finding**; **(12)** every recommendation states mandatory-for-conformance / strengthening / informative / future-methodology-candidate, **preventing accidental normative force**; **(13)** self-audit changes log original + revised classification + governing rule + reasoning, so **review history becomes methodology evidence rather than hidden editorial evolution.** **APPLIED RETROACTIVELY to the IBC-1 review (§7 addendum) — and the result CHANGES WHAT THE VERDICT MEANS: ZERO ARCHITECTURE DEFECTS. Of 21 findings: 0 architecture defects · 12 governance defects · 1 methodology observation · 6 improvement recommendations · 1 question. Nothing in IBC-1 asserts wrong architecture — every defect is a failure of TRANSMISSION (misstating the baseline's status, omitting recorded content, stating as settled what the record left open). Under the old single-severity list this was invisible: "3 Critical, 11 Major" reads as *the contract is substantially wrong*, when the accurate reading is *substantially incomplete, with wrong status claims*. Merging severity and category MISDESCRIBED THE ARTIFACT.** Concrete reclassifications: **IBC-m3 SPLIT** (Rule 9) into an improvement recommendation + a **methodology observation** — the T-17 limb *is not IBC-1's to resolve*, and merging them made a routing error look like a drafting error · **IBC-S2 → QUESTION** (Rule 11, resolving act indeterminable) · m2/m5/S1/M11 → improvement recommendations · **IBC-m4's constitutional weight RAISED to baseline-basis governance defect while its severity stayed Minor — the orthogonality demonstrated directly.** Rule 12 applied to all 13 recommendations (9 mandatory-for-conformance · 2 strengthening · 2 informative · 0 methodology candidates). Rule 10 verified: no recommendation rewrites authoritative-source wording. **Verdict REVISE unchanged, meaning now precise: revise for transmission accuracy and completeness, NOT for architecture.** **PMR-5 REFINED (Chief Architect's three-level formulation folded): L1 governed terms — never paraphrase · L2 normative rules — never paraphrase independently, amend the source · L3 explanatory prose — may paraphrase if meaning preserved AND marked. This RESOLVES BOTH questions PMR-5 originally raised** (VR-2 was an L1 violation; M8 Finding 1 an L2 item — different levels the single-level formulation could not express; and L3 answers the marked-paraphrase question, removing the over-restriction risk since M8's own existence depends on being allowed to summarize). **One narrower question remains: WHO assigns a passage's level, and when — recommendation recorded that level is a property of the SOURCE's own marking, assigned when the source is written, not inferred downstream, or the rule relocates the judgment it exists to remove.**
- **⬛ ARB REVIEW DISCIPLINE — RULES 14/16/17 ADOPTED, RULE 15 ASSESSED AND DECLINED (Chief Architect, 2026-07-28).** The instruction set attached its own caution — *new dimensions only where they repeatedly distinguish classes previously conflated; confidence and evidence-origin adopted only if they demonstrably improve review quality over multiple cycles* — **so the caution was applied to the four rules being drafted.** **ADOPTED: Rule 14** (declare review scope before findings; **verdicts apply only within scope**) **and Rule 17** (summaries report by constitutional category first, severity second) — **because neither adds taxonomy: both are reporting rules for Rule 9's already-adopted dimension, and §7.1 had already adopted the category-first presentation spontaneously.** **Rule 16** (evidence origin: artifact-local / cross-artifact / reviewer knowledge) **adopted on one cycle's concrete yield**, with the multi-cycle standard **recorded as not yet met.** **DECLINED: Rule 15** (per-finding confidence) — **n = 0 against the Authority's own standard.** Across four review cycles and three self-audits there is **not one instance** where a confidence label would have changed a classification, severity, or routing: every apparent case had a structural cause exposed by **authority basis** (IBC-M11), **resolving act** (IBC-S2), **category** (IBC-m3), or **external evidence** (AFV-F1/F3). Held as **PMR-6** with the theoretical case recorded fairly (a reviewer's only current outlet for uncertainty is lowering *severity* — the very conflation Rule 9 prevents), a **duplication risk** flagged (the program already grades confidence for *domain* claims via M0's rubric and MCR-5; a finding's correctness is largely determined by its authority basis, leaving little room for a middle grade), and a **re-assessment trigger** named. **PMR-6 is the first candidate in the register assessed and DECLINED on the Authority's own evidential standard rather than merely held.** **APPLIED to the IBC-1 review (§8): SCOPED VERDICT now explicit — Architecture PASS · Governance REVISE · Methodology OBSERVATION · Documentation REVISE · Implementation OUT OF SCOPE** (the unscoped "REVISE" had invited the reading *architecturally wrong*; scoped, a revising author knows which sections to touch). **Rule 16 produced a NEW result no other dimension surfaced: 0 reviewer-knowledge findings — the review is reproducible by anyone with repository access — but only 6 of 21 are ARTIFACT-LOCAL, so IBC-1 read in isolation yields under a third of them.** That is a fact about the *contract*, not the review: **a boundary contract should be checkable against itself (its own §11 presupposes it) and this one largely is not, because it states categories where the record states content — Rule 16 independently corroborates the systemic finding by a different route.** Consequence recorded: **conformance criteria evaluable only by a reviewer holding fifteen other artifacts are not criteria an implementation team can self-assess**, so Recommendations 4–8 would raise the artifact-local proportion substantially — a benefit not previously stated.

Now: usable and encouraged, not yet permanent, with **explicit promotion and demotion conditions recorded** (promote: yields information no other dimension surfaces in ≥1 further independent cycle; demote: two cycles where its classifications are derivable from Rules 8/9/11 alone) — mirroring the provisional-certification pattern. **(2) MEMORY.md STOPPED BECOMING A SECOND METHODOLOGY DOCUMENT:** the 18-rule text EXTRACTED to a dedicated reviewer playbook — **`docs/implementation/PKS_ARB_Review_Discipline.md`** (Parts I–V: scope · classification · source-of-truth · reporting · provisional/declined, each rule carrying its admission evidence) — and MEMORY reduced to a **pointer** (the repo's own established POINTER pattern; MEMORY holds durable state, not procedures). **(3) RULE 18 ADOPTED into the playbook:** every declared review scope shall identify its **governing artifacts**, and no finding within that scope shall exceed their authority — completing the chain **scope → authority → evidence → finding → constitutional act.** Admission evidence satisfied the ARB's own filter: IBC-1 §8.1's scoped verdicts carried no evidential anchor (a real gap in delivered work, not a hypothetical). **THE ADMISSION FILTER ITSELF ADOPTED as the playbook's growth rule, binding on the document's own future: *"did a real defect escape because this rule was missing? If not, don't add it."*** Reproducibility retained permanently as a first-class governance property (stronger than "evidence-based": anyone with repository access can re-derive the review).

- **⭐ GOVERNING PRINCIPLE RECORDED (Integrity Model): *the PROPOSITION is primary; the IDENTIFIER is a REFERENCE to it — not the other way around. Where they diverge, the identifier retires WITH its proposition rather than carrying a falsified premise forward under a familiar name.*** **The auditability test that settled it: *an annotation preserves HISTORY but not SEMANTIC CLARITY — and an identifier whose meaning requires a chain of nested exceptions has stopped functioning as a reference.*** **⚠️ Not in conflict with identifier STABILITY: stability forbids REASSIGNING an identifier to different content (clarify → alias → annotate, never renumber); this governs whether an identifier TRAVELS with a changed proposition. *Never renumber — but do not stretch either. - **⭐ THREE KINDS OF CHANGE consolidated (Integrity Model), ordered by breadth: **STATUS** (how strongly the same proposition is held → the classification ladder) · **RATIONALE** (the GROUNDS for the same outcome → *a rationale change is itself a governance change*; instances AFV-F4 and CDR-1 §6) · **ONTOLOGY** (what the finding is ABOUT → a NEW identifier; instance CON-F2→M7-F1).** ***Each is easy to mistake for the one above it, and a SUBSTITUTION presented as a STATUS CHANGE is the most dangerous because it looks like housekeeping.*** *The Authority's dependency observation made this legible: limb (b) was falsified BY DEPENDENCY on limb (a), so the finding was dismantled at the level of its LOGICAL STRUCTURE, not merely re-graded.*

Operative: ***the ONTOLOGY CHANGE is the conceptual event; the new identifier is a GOVERNANCE CONSEQUENCE that NORMALLY follows.*** *The hedge is load-bearing — a proposition can change without any successor being warranted, and where the observation does not survive at all the finding simply dissolves with nothing following it.*
- **⭐ THE FOUR CONCEPTS recorded (Integrity Model) as the axis making the three-tier model coherent: **1 EVIDENCE** (what is OBSERVED) · **2 PROPOSITION** (what is CLAIMED) · **3 RATIONALE** (why it is JUSTIFIED) · **4 DISPOSITION** (what governance DECIDES). The three changes operate on the middle two — status = strength of 2 · rationale = 3 leaving 2 and 4 intact · ontology = 2 itself, so 4 must be retaken.** ***Every conflation this programme has caught was a collapse between TWO ADJACENT LEVELS: strengthened-vs-confirmed collapsed 1 into 2 · SDM v1.1's authority claim collapsed 3 into 4 · CON-F2's "reclassification" collapsed 2 into a status change.***

- **📘 EVIDENCE INFORMS a decision; it does not REPLACE one (Integrity Model): where a question is a governance CHOICE, ***no evidence ALONE can settle it*** — but evidence supplies the available interpretations, their consequences, and their compatibility with existing rules. *My weaker "no evidence I can gather would settle it" implied evidence is useless where it is merely insufficient.*

## 🔒 PHASE II: COMPLETE AND FROZEN (2026-07-31)

**`PKS_Phase_II_Baseline_Freeze.md`** — no further refinement except through **formal change control**. Binds SDM v1.2/EOP v1.2, the governance framework, the six promoted artifacts, and every Phase II terminal state. ***Reopening requires NEW EVIDENCE or a NEW MANDATE, never a re-reading of the same record.*** **NOT a prohibition on change · NOT a claim of correctness — *a frozen baseline is settled, not proven* · NOT a closure of the live deferrals.**

## 🏁 PHASE III: CHARTERED (2026-07-31)
**Authority roadmap recorded:** charter operational validation → implement against SDM v1.2/EOP v1.2 → gather operational evidence → observe real governance usage → open targeted commissions only when new evidence requires them.

**⚠️ The charter is NOT written. *Phase III needs "a new mandate, not 'continue Phase II'", and a mandate is the Authority's to issue.* Two of its four elements are substantive Authority content that cannot be inferred: WHAT OPERATIONAL EVIDENCE IS WANTED and SUCCESS CRITERIA.** *On a mandate, the structure can be drafted immediately; without one, writing it would be Phase II continuing under a new name.*

**New default: *backlog items wait until they have a CONSTITUTIONAL REASON TO MOVE.*** **Burden shift: *once a governance baseline is constitutionally complete, the burden moves from REFINING THE GOVERNANCE to DEMONSTRATING ITS VALUE IN PRACTICE.***

**`PKS_Phase_III_Operational_Validation_Charter.md` ISSUED** — a NEW GOVERNANCE ACT, not a continuation of Phase II.

**Guiding principle:** ***internal soundness establishes NOTHING about whether the methodology WORKS.*** Operational Evidence is at ZERO-INDEPENDENT because *a certification dimension derives credibility from being outside the unilateral control of the programme it certifies — only USE can move it.*

**⚠️ TWO GUARDS added under the programme's own discipline: (1) the success criteria must ADMIT NEGATIVE FINDINGS — every criterion in the mandate is written in the improvement direction, and *a validation that can only observe success is not a validation*; each negative form is now stated (incl. ***reviews SLOWER or MORE OPAQUE than before the governance existed***). (2) They are OBSERVATIONS, NOT TARGETS — *a criterion treated as a target can be met by changing behaviour to satisfy the measure.***

**HONEST FAILURE DIRECTION named in advance:** ***the baseline's likeliest failure is not being WRONG but being TOO EXPENSIVE FOR THE VALUE IT DELIVERS — correct governance no one can afford to follow.***

**Operating rules:** baseline FROZEN · **backlog items WAIT for a constitutional reason to move** · deferrals stay deferred · administrative work is now normal maintenance · ⛔ ***if a Phase III act produces a governance artifact without producing OPERATIONAL EVIDENCE, that act has FAILED THE MANDATE regardless of the artifact's quality.***

**Operational use may begin. The next governance commission should be triggered by EVIDENCE FROM OPERATIONAL WORK — not by this charter and not by the backlog.**

**MISSION (as adopted):** *KnowledgeOS exists to improve the delivery of PublicDigit by governing how engineering work is planned, approved, executed, verified and evolved — in a form independent of project, language and execution provider.*

**8 responsibilities · 10 non-responsibilities**, each traced. ⭐ Notable non-responsibilities: **N-5 never approve its own work** *("it does not approve itself")* · **N-6 automation never defines governance** · **N-10 no orchestrators/workflow engines/state machines** *("deliberately, and not forever")*. ⛔ **N-1 "never own business knowledge" is BREACHED by ES-005.1.**

### ⭐⭐⭐ CANONICAL META-MODEL — derived, not invented; one proposal rejected on its own rule

**`docs/knowledgeos/KnowledgeOS_Meta_Model.md`** (CANDIDATE). ⭐ **Derived from `RQ-002_Knowledge_Meta_Model` (COMPLETE, unadopted) + the executable L-A schema — checked BEFORE deriving, per the recurring-error lesson.**

**⛔ CONCEDED: `INTENDED` WITHDRAWN.** *Intent is not evidence — and the repository's own rule condemns it (**CLAUDE.md**: "a new dimension, an **overloaded existing dimension**, or merely another value"). **I added it inside a document about evidence discipline.***
**⭐ ADOPTED: *"Mission is ENACTED"*** replaces *"implicit"* — the platform is not describing its mission, it is **already behaving according to it**.

**⭐ SIX DIMENSIONS — 4 existing, 2 new, 1 REJECTED:**

| Dimension | Source | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| **NATURE** · **REPRESENTATION** · **GOVERNANCE-STATUS** · **AUTHORITY-SCOPE** | ⭐ **RQ-002 + `statuses.yaml` + ES-005.1/placement scopes** | ⭐ **ADMIT — exist** |
| ⭐ **EVIDENCE-STATUS** *(observed/inferred/hypothesized)* | ⛔ not a field anywhere | ⭐ **ADMIT — NEW.** *Ten rediscoveries + four falsifications happened because claims were ungraded* |
| ⭐ **OPERATIONAL-STATE** *(enforced/advisory/unexercised)* | ⛔ implied by `deny`/`ask`/hooks, never formalized | ⭐ **ADMIT — NEW.** *It is what "0 traversals" and my "0 of 11 enforcing" error both measured badly* |
| ⛔⛔ **INTENT-STATUS** | proposed by the reviewer | ⛔ **REJECT — fails orthogonality** |

> ⛔⛔ **WHY INTENT-STATUS IS REJECTED — the reviewer's own rule, applied consistently:** `statuses.yaml` already runs draft→discovery→reviewed→approved→baseline→frozen→superseded→archived. **"Ratified" IS "approved"; "proposed" IS "draft"; "operational" IS "baseline".** ⭐ **It decomposes: `INTENT = f(GOVERNANCE-STATUS, OWNER)`, and both fields already exist.** *Rejecting `INTENDED` for overloading evidence while accepting `INTENT-STATUS` overloading governance would have been selective.*

```
KnowledgeOS instance = NATURE × REPRESENTATION × GOVERNANCE-STATUS × AUTHORITY-SCOPE
                       × EVIDENCE-STATUS × OPERATIONAL-STATE
DERIVED, never axes:  lifecycle = f(NATURE) · validation = f(REPRESENTATION, NATURE)
                      intent    = f(GOVERNANCE-STATUS, OWNER)
```

**⭐ SIX CONCEPTS** by responsibility/lifecycle/ownership/dependencies: **MISSION** *(sponsor; enacted→candidate→ratified)* · **ENGINEERING KNOWLEDGE** · **ENGINEERING CAPABILITY** *(protects exactly ONE invariant)* · **ENGINEERING ARTIFACT** · **PKS** · **PRODUCT**.

> # ⭐⭐⭐ **THE LOAD-BEARING EDGE: `ENGINEERING KNOWLEDGE` ≠ `ENGINEERING ARTIFACT`**
> **This makes my three-times-repeated error UNSTATABLE.** *You can no longer say "X does not exist" — only "**no ARTIFACT carries X**", which is a REPRESENTATION claim, not a NATURE claim.*
> ⭐ **A meta-model earns its place by making a known error class impossible to phrase. This one does.**
> ⭐ **And documents are now the LOWEST layer of the model, not its centre** (the reviewer's §8, adopted structurally).

**⭐ SUCCESS CRITERION TESTED on six real artifacts — all classify without altering the model.** ⭐⭐ *And the test produced a finding by itself:* **`Round39-MC` is `frozen` yet ENFORCED, while CAP-001 is `realized` yet only ADVISORY** — **proving GOVERNANCE-STATUS ⟂ OPERATIONAL-STATE by use rather than by argument.**

> # ⭐⭐ **THE DURABLE OUTPUT: 11 INVARIANTS vs 8 IMPLEMENTATION CHOICES**
> **A future adopter may change every choice and none of the invariants.** *That is what makes this a platform rather than a repository layout.*
>
> **Invariants include:** ⭐ **I-1 Knowledge is REPRESENTED by an Artifact; an Artifact is never the Knowledge** · I-2 a Capability protects exactly ONE invariant · I-3 a Knowledge Space has a DECLARED boundary · I-4 a Projection is `derived` and cited as authority by nothing · I-5 one authoritative artifact per topic *(lint-enforced)* · I-6 the Runtime never owns Knowledge · ⛔ **I-7 a PKS never contains reusable Method — STATED AND BREACHED** · I-9 status ⟂ authority · I-10 constitutional principles are void-overriding · ⭐ **I-11 Knowledge is NEVER GENERATIVE** *(only Capabilities, Spaces and Products generate — the asymmetry that keeps knowledge inert and auditable)*.
> **Choices include:** folders · markdown · YAML · Mermaid · **the graph's declared scope** · Claude Code as runtime · frontmatter · identifier formats.

**⭐ SUCCESS CRITERION TESTED on 11 real things — all place, NO new top-level concept needed.** ⭐⭐ *And the test surfaced a candidate invariant by itself:* **I-12 — a Capability may READ across a space boundary but never OWN what it reads** *(CAP-001 is platform-side and reads product-side registers)*. **Recorded as hypothesis H-1, n=1, not admitted.**

**→ OPEN: RO-1 adopt `DP-n` as a named layer · RO-2 are Knowledge Spaces nested or adjacent · RO-3 repair or withdraw the breached I-7 · RO-4 the Runtime Adapter has no declared owner · MM-1 adopt RQ-002 · MQ-1 ratify the enacted mission.**


### ⛔⛔ CROSS-PRODUCT VALIDATION — the ontology SURVIVES, but NOT UNCHANGED

**`docs/knowledgeos/KnowledgeOS_Ontology_Cross_Product_Validation.md`.** Projected into **PublicDigit (actual) · Hospital Information System · ERP System**. ⛔ **The ontology was HELD FIXED throughout.**

**Result: 8 of 12 concepts · 10 of 11 invariants survived. ⛔ One falsified, one failed, one disappeared.**

> # ⛔⛔ **`I-4` FALSIFIED — and it hits CAP-002**
> **"A projection is `derived` and is cited as authority by nothing."** ⛔ **FALSE as a general rule:** a **discharge summary** and a **financial statement** are both **GENERATED and AUTHORITATIVE** — signed, filed, cited by clinicians, insurers, courts, auditors.
> ⭐ **Diagnosis: `derived` describes PROVENANCE; `authoritative` describes STANDING. They are INDEPENDENT. A signed derivation is both.**
> ⚠️ **This is the exact wording of DP-2, which governs CAP-002 Projection Integrity.**

> # ⛔ **"ONE PKS PER PRODUCT" FAILED**
> *The same ERP deploys to fifty customers with different charts of accounts, fiscal calendars and jurisdictions.* ⛔ **The model cannot distinguish a PRODUCT from a DEPLOYMENT of it.** ⚠️ *PublicDigit masks this — it HAS multi-tenancy (`organisation_id`) in code and the ontology treats it as one product.*

> # ⚠️ **THE AUTHORITY CHAIN HAS NO PLACE FOR EXTERNAL AUTHORITY**
> *HIPAA/GDPR, IFRS/GAAP — principles the organisation must **obey** and does **not own**.* ⛔ **The ontology can express *who decides*; it cannot express *who must be obeyed*.** ⚠️ *Masked in PublicDigit, where election law is treated as product domain knowledge.*

**⭐⭐ THE SYSTEMIC GAP — all four missing relationships share ONE shape:**
`contains · references · generates · depends on` are **STRUCTURAL**. ⛔ **`specializes · constrains · instantiates · attests` are TRANSFORMATIONAL — and the ontology has none of them.**
> ⭐ ***The ontology describes COMPOSITION. It does not describe TRANSFORMATION.*** *One coherent gap, not four unrelated ones.*

**⭐ Three candidate specializations recorded, none admitted:** PRINCIPLE *(internal / **external**)* · PRODUCT *(product / **deployment**)* · PROJECTION *(**inert** / **attested**)*.

**⭐ CONFIRMED BY DISAPPEARANCE:** `Runtime Adapter` vanished in both new domains — **empirical support for keeping it outside the core ontology.**
**⭐⭐ `DESIGN POLICY` held in ALL THREE** — *the layer discovered last projected best.*
**⭐ `I-11` restated to "Knowledge does not EXECUTE"** — ⭐ **the correction SURVIVED projection where my original wording would have failed.**

> # ⭐⭐ **THE METHODOLOGICAL RESULT: PUBLICDIGIT MASKED TWO OF THE THREE FAILURES.**
> **Multi-tenancy and external legal authority both EXIST in PublicDigit and are both INVISIBLE in the model.** ⛔ *A single-product validation could never have found them — cross-product projection is now justified by **evidence**, not by principle.*

**→ OPEN: CV-1 is `I-4` scoped or withdrawn (DP-2/CAP-002 states the falsified form) · CV-2 PRODUCT vs DEPLOYMENT · CV-3 where does EXTERNAL authority attach · CV-4 admit the four transformational relationships as a GROUP · CV-5 adopt "Strategic Ontology Candidate" status.**


### ⭐⭐⭐ LIFECYCLE GAP ANALYSIS — only 1 of 4 "ontology failures" was ontological

**`docs/knowledgeos/KnowledgeOS_Lifecycle_Gap_Analysis.md`.** ⛔ **Ontology FROZEN throughout.**

**⛔ CONCEDED: `I-4` IS NOT FALSIFIED. I misdiagnosed a LIFECYCLE gap as an ONTOLOGY falsification — my FOURTH level confusion** *(flat-vs-dimensional → artifact-vs-concept → layer-vs-sibling → **lifecycle-vs-structure**)*.
*A discharge summary is not authoritative **because it was projected** — it became authoritative via `DERIVED → REVIEWED → ATTESTED → RELEASED`. **Governance created the authority.*** ⭐ **So DP-2 does not state a falsehood — it CONFLATES DERIVATION WITH ATTESTATION.** *Both prior documents annotated.*

**⭐ A FIFTH "X does not exist" CLAIM WAS AVOIDED BY CHECKING.** `docs/knowledge/_meta/lifecycle.md` (`status: baseline`, `authority: authoritative`, v1.1) already supplies:
- §1 **two independent dimensions** — *"most projects wrongly conflate them"*
- §2 **a WHO-MAY-MOVE-IT role table** for every status transition *(Author · Peer reviewer · ARB/Chief Architect · Knowledge/Release Manager · Governance Board · **ADR-only** for frozen→superseded)*
- §3 **transition GUARDS** — *"each transition has guards `knowledge-lint` can check"*
- §4 **Knowledge Quality Gates** — *"'Approved' is objective"*
**Plus EIGHT more lifecycles** *(statuses.yaml · ES-006.1 ladder · EEP · capability · ADR-M status · methodology maturity · 4 identifier lifecycles · Observation Protocol)*.

> # ⭐⭐⭐ **THE GAP, LOCATED MECHANICALLY IN TWO YAML FILES**
> | | `statuses.yaml` | `authorities.yaml` |
> |---|---|---|
> | progression field | ⭐ **`order`** | ⛔ **none** |
> | terminal marker | ⭐ **`settled`** | ⛔ **none** |
> | who may move it | ⭐ **§2 role table** | ⛔⛔ **NOT DEFINED** |
> | transition guards | ⭐ **§3, lint-checkable** | ⛔⛔ **NOT DEFINED** |
>
> ### **STATUS HAS A GOVERNED STATE MACHINE. AUTHORITY DOES NOT.** ⭐ *Attestation **is** an authority transition (`derived → authoritative`). Because authority transitions are ungoverned, "attested" is inexpressible — so the model collapses "derived" into "never authoritative".*
> ⭐⭐ **One asymmetry between two YAML files explains the single most consequential failure in the cross-product validation.**

**⭐ CLASSIFICATION — the commission's hypothesis CONFIRMED:**

| Gap | Category | Cause | Belongs to |
|---|---|---|---|
| **G-1 `I-4`/ATTESTS** | **EVOLUTIONARY** | **missing LIFECYCLE** | ⭐⭐ **LIFECYCLE** |
| **G-2 `SPECIALIZES`** | **EVOLUTIONARY** | missing relationship | ⭐ **ONTOLOGY** *(the only one)* |
| **G-3 external authority** | **BEHAVIORAL** | missing relationship — *obligation ≠ ownership* | ⭐ **GOVERNANCE** |
| ⭐ **G-4 PRODUCT vs DEPLOYMENT** | STRUCTURAL | ⭐⭐ **DISSOLVES — a deployment IS a nested Knowledge Space** | **resolved, no new concept** |
| **G-5 Runtime Adapter vanished** | STRUCTURAL | ⛔ **not a failure — a CONFIRMATION** | — |

> ⭐⭐ **ONLY 1 OF 4 IS GENUINELY ONTOLOGICAL.** *Most of what looked like ontology failure was lifecycle and governance wearing ontology's clothes.*
> ⭐ **G-4 closes `H-3` on evidence: Knowledge Spaces NEST, and a deployment is the nested case.**

**⚠️ NEW OBSERVATION found while checking:** `lifecycle.md`'s §1 diagram shows **`idea → research → draft → …`** but `statuses.yaml` **does not define `idea` or `research`** — ⛔ **they exist as `knowledge_type` values instead.** *A type/status conflation inside the very document whose §1 warns against exactly that.* ⚠️ *Recorded as an observation, not a defect claim.*
**⚠️ Five lifecycle vocabularies — two pairs declared orthogonal (HEALTHY), two pairs possibly duplicated:** statuses.yaml(8) vs ADR-M(5); ES-006.1(4) vs methodology maturity(5).

**→ OPEN: LG-1 should `authority` get a governed state machine with roles and guards · LG-2 restate DP-2 to separate derivation from attestation · LG-3 is `idea`/`research` a status or a type · LG-4 two axes or two names · LG-5 admit `SPECIALIZES` · LG-6 where does obligation attach.**


### ⭐⭐⭐ ENGINEERING PROGRESSION MODEL — LG-1 DISSOLVED, and `authority` decomposed

**`docs/knowledgeos/KnowledgeOS_Engineering_Progression_Model.md`** (CANDIDATE). ⛔ **Pure discovery — ontology, governance, lifecycle, both YAMLs frozen.**

**⛔ CONCEDED: LG-1 was SOLUTION DESIGN** — *"missing transitions → need state machine" infers a model from a missing implementation, which DDD forbids.* **Withdrawn as ill-posed.**
**⭐ CONFIRMED: the reviewer's "that chain is QUALIFICATION"** — the repository already owns the vocabulary (ES-006.1's `qualification` stage · ES-003) **and already records a UL collision on the word**, which now carries a third candidate sense.

> # ⭐⭐⭐ **FLAGSHIP: `authority` IS TWO DIMENSIONS IN ONE FIELD — AND ITS OWN HEADER SAYS SO**
> *`authorities.yaml`: "how much should I trust this **and** where did it come from?"* — **TWO questions, ONE field.**
>
> | Values | Kind | Can it change? |
> |---|---|---|
> | `generated` · `derived` | ⭐⭐ **PROVENANCE** | ⛔⛔ **NEVER — origin is an immutable historical fact** |
> | `authoritative` · `provisional` *("no authority **yet**")* · `historical` | ⭐ **STANDING** | ✅ **by a GOVERNANCE ACT** |
>
> ### ⭐⭐ **WHY `authority` HAS NO STATE MACHINE: half of it CANNOT have one, and the other half changes by ACTS — which the platform records as act-logs, not workflows.** *The asymmetry is now EXPLAINED, not repaired.*
> ### ⭐ **"What is authority?" answered: an immutable PROVENANCE fact × a mutable STANDING judgment. Attestation changes standing. Nothing changes provenance.**
> *Also explains I-4's persistence: a financial statement is `derived` in provenance and `authoritative` in standing — **simultaneously, without contradiction**.*

**⭐ FOUR KINDS OF PROGRESSION + ONE NON-PROGRESSION** *(10 mechanisms answered against the 7 commissioned questions)*:
| Kind | Moved by | Instances |
|---|---|---|
| **GOVERNANCE-ACT** | a decision by a named role | status · ADR-M · identifiers · standing-changes |
| ⭐⭐ **EVIDENTIAL** | **accumulating evidence — nobody can decree it** | methodology maturity · evidence n-counts |
| **WORK-EXECUTION** | time; has a clock | EEP · Observation Protocol |
| **COMPOSITE trajectory** | crosses the above | ES-006.1 ladder · capability lifecycle |
| ⛔ **PROVENANCE (non-progression)** | **nothing — immutable** | half of `authority` |

> # ⭐⭐ **THE PLATFORM'S CENTRAL MECHANISM, NAMED AT LAST:**
> ### **evidence EARNS · governance GRANTS · promotion requires BOTH.**
> *No ruling can make an observation `Replicated`. No evidence can make a document `frozen`.*

**⭐ LG-4 answered by KIND:** status vs ADR-M = **same kind, two instances** (parsimony question) · ES-006.1 vs maturity = **different kinds, correctly separate** (composite-with-gate vs purely evidential).
**⭐ Coupling by GUARD is healthy:** `baseline` requires `authority ∈ {authoritative, derived}` — dimensions stay separate; a transition in one consults the other.
**⭐ Prior art honoured:** GEP-F1's two-lifecycle rule + two declared orthogonalities = **three prior refusals of lifecycle fusion. This extends canon; it does not found it.**

**→ OPEN: PM-1 separate authority's two questions (supersedes LG-1) · PM-2 role/guard treatment for standing-changes · PM-3 one vocabulary for status+ADR-M · PM-4 rule on the "Qualification" collision (third sense now) · PM-5 model capability as an explicit composite · LG-5 admit SPECIALIZES.**

### ⛔ STANDING RULE (adopted 2026-08-03 — this entry is its adoption record and the LAST of its kind)

> **No governance-only state updates without a governance event.**
> ⭐ **A governance event is any event that CHANGES THE GOVERNANCE STATE of the platform** *(refined 2026-08-03 — definition over enumeration)*. Examples, not an exhaustive list: **a DA ruling · a new OE entry · a commission trigger firing · a multi-product validation result.** Not governance events: wording refinements · interpretation refinements · naming discussions · better framings — governance must not become self-referential.

**Two corrections folded in (chat/session-log wording):** architecture is downstream of **EVIDENCE**, not of engineering (it reacts to validated evidence, never to every event) · the next word belongs to **OPERATIONAL EVIDENCE**, not to "the work" (engineering alone changes nothing; only its evidence does). **Broadened characterization accepted:** what was built is *a governance system that controls the promotion of engineering knowledge* — preventing premature architecture is one consequence, not the purpose.

**Closing report (reviewer's text, recorded as the commission's epitaph):** *"Strategic Discovery Commission 1 has fulfilled its mandate. The resulting architecture is the operational baseline, not an active design space. Future evolution is driven by operational evidence; new discovery occurs only through explicitly chartered commissions opened in response to validated evidence."*

**Tomorrow's only question:** *"What engineering work are we doing next, and what operational evidence might it generate?"*

### OE-KOS-3 review: new candidate principle OI-1; two futures shelved (2026-08-04) — state + pointers only

- **Shelved with convergence pointers:** DoD-as-merge-contract inversion (→ already-routed Layer-3/4 advisory→enforcing gate; this is its fuller form) · **Verification Engine** (policy→engine→checks; reminders become implementations of one capability) → ⭐ **converges with the Cloud idea's rules-as-data governance engine — the same engine seen from the local side.** Neither built; the reviewer's own "hasn't earned itself yet" is the gate.


### OI-1 refined (2026-08-04) — state + pointers only

- **OI-1 REWORDED (survives longer):** *every governance obligation should have a DEFINED VERIFICATION MECHANISM* — mechanisms include executable checks · human review · periodic audits · measurements · observations; **instrumentation is ONE implementation strategy, not the principle.**
- **Hierarchy hypothesis recorded (reviewer's, graded as interpretation):** OI-1 as the PARENT — EV-1 · DG-1 · test-presence · ADR checks as specializations beneath it. *Plausible (OI-1 explains why EV-1 exists); the ladder decides structure, not the shelf.*
- ⭐ **The layering distinction preserved verbatim:** `Governance Rules → Verification Mechanisms → Runtime Instrumentation → Observations` — **the Verification-Mechanisms layer is NOT runtime instrumentation**; the policy exists independently of every runtime (the deep principle: *policy → verification → instrumentation → runtime, never reminder → policy*).
- Verification Engine: shelf confirmed — today it would wrap four shell scripts; it becomes the simplest architecture only when independent obligations multiply.

Recorded so status never drifts.

### ⛔ ARB CLOSING RESOLUTION — the architecture phase ends (chair's review, 2026-08-04)

The evidence hierarchy *(Engineering Event → Observation → Evidence → Assessment → Learning)* now outranks any individual bounded context — it governs how knowledge is produced.

- **The ONE remaining architectural uncertainty, recorded verbatim:** *"Will developers actually change their behavior because of KnowledgeOS?"* — everything else has supporting implementation; this has only TIME. It is the behavioural success criterion the spike plan already carries ("first developer changes code because of a warning"), now elevated to the programme's single open question.
- ⭐ **Monthly ARB review = five charts; coverage CHECKED against dashboard v2: 4 of 5 already render** — funnel ✓ (issued/decided/accepted/ignored/deferred) · response latency ✓ (lead times, recommendation→decision) · assessment distribution ✓ (verdict columns per rule) · per-rule effectiveness ✓. **EIS is the sole gap — deliberately: population = 1 intervention; it renders when evidence exists, never before.** The monthly review can run off the existing dashboard today.
- **The future DDD milestone STAGED, not planned:** Recommendation/Decision/Outcome/Assessment will either remain application-level workflow objects or reveal a genuine bounded context — **engineering will force the question; the decision is postponed until operational evidence exists** *(already the approved plan's stance — confirmed, not created)*.

- ⭐ **The named achievement is a PROPERTY, not a component: the system is FALSIFIABLE** — it can demonstrate a recommendation was ignored, an accepted one produced no improvement, a heuristic is ineffective, or that KnowledgeOS itself does not improve engineering.
- Closing recommendation held: *treat every recommendation as an experiment, every decision as evidence, every assessment as knowledge, every month as an opportunity to validate — or falsify — the current model.*
